- **shop-blueprint-generator**: Generates heavily dimensioned, technical orthographic blueprints intended strictly for carpenters and fabricators. All angles are READ from structure.json — NEVER derived mentally. ([richfrem/garden-structure-designer](https://github.com/richfrem/garden-structure-designer/tree/HEAD/plugins/garden-structure-designer/skills/shop-blueprint-generator) · ★1)
- **shop-board**: Turn any online shop into a calm, visual board in the browser: the whole catalog as a photo grid, filtered to your sizes, grouped by intent instead of product type, click to pick, then add the picks to the store's cart. Use for "make a board for &lt;shop&gt;", "go through &lt;store&gt; for me", "find me stuff on &lt;store&gt;", "review my cart", or any browse-a-whole-store / trim-a-cart ask.
